# Worklog

### 2026-06-25
- [x] Changed inactive-BOT detection from per-user allocation reporting to BOT-level reporting: upload/live BOTs are only flagged when every assigned user is inactive beyond the threshold.
- [x] Kept live BOTs with non-terminal live streams, including waiting/standby 24/7 streams, out of inactive alerts because the BOT is still doing work.
- [x] Updated Telegram inactive-BOT messages to list BOT-level details without enumerating each assigned user.
- [x] Fixed missing admin user avatars caused by invisible prefix characters in usernames; `_initials()` now picks the first alphanumeric character instead of blindly using the first codepoint.
- [x] Fixed the remaining admin avatar regression for users hashed to `bg-rose-500`: added the missing Tailwind safelist class, rebuilt `admin-tailwind.css`, and bumped the CSS cache key.

### 2026-06-24
- [x] Reworked shell navigation to use browser document prefetch and instant active-state updates instead of custom HTML fetch + page fade.
- [x] Replaced CDN/deferred Lucide hydration on admin/user shells with local SVG runtimes and server-rendered shell icons, preserving current icon shapes, sizes, and stroke widths.
- [x] Changed user render table, user live table, and admin BOT polling to keyed row reconciliation so unchanged rows/icons stay in place during polling.
- [x] Kept create/delete/destructive flows backend-confirmed with existing confirmation semantics; no aggressive optimistic deletion or job mutation.
- [x] Verified locally with `python -m pytest -q`, `python -m pytest tests\test_navigation_performance.py -q`, `node --check backend\app\static\js\admin_navigation.js`, `node --check backend\app\static\js\user_dashboard.js`, and `python -m compileall -q backend\app`.

### 2026-06-02
- [x] Sửa guard failover cho live `24/7` có backup: `runtime_health` chỉ đi qua live progress route, upload progress không nhận field live-only; backup hot-standby chỉ stream sau khi primary RTMP unhealthy vượt 30 giây hoặc runtime disconnected thật sự vượt ngưỡng telemetry dài hơn, không còn bị kích hoạt bởi retry transient. Worker retry health signal nếu gửi fail và chỉ báo primary healthy sau khi progress ổn định ngắn.
- [x] Quay lại pattern live push legacy sau khi đối chiếu app cũ và log reset theo vòng media: `_build_live_ffmpeg_arguments()` không còn thêm `-stream_loop -1`; FFmpeg đẩy một vòng `rendered.flv`, sau đó worker respawn gần như ngay lập tức khi hết media hoặc YouTube/RTMPS reset (`WORKER_LIVE_RTMP_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS=0`). Timed live primary+backup vẫn chạy song song; `24/7` backup vẫn hot-standby/failover.
- [x] Xoá hẳn nhánh thử nghiệm FIFO khỏi `live_runner.py` và test để production worker không còn đường bật `WORKER_LIVE_RTMP_FIFO_ENABLED`; nếu sau này muốn thử recovery khác thì phải làm bằng thay đổi code/test riêng.

### 2026-05-31
- [x] Compared the legacy `LiveStreamAsync()` loop: the old worker did not whitelist RTMP error strings; after each FFmpeg live exit it logged the exit and immediately started another FFmpeg process until stop/end.
- [x] Updated the new live runner to retry every `FFmpeg live runtime failed (...)` from the RTMP phase, including `24/7` primary streams that have backup, while keeping non-FFmpeg errors terminal.
- [x] Verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_live_rtmp_retry.py tests\test_live_runtime_lease.py` and `python -m compileall workers\agent backend\app`.

### 2026-05-29
- [x] Changed the admin BOT `Không hoạt động` column to use a daily snapshot refreshed after the 8h inactive-BOT alert window instead of recalculating on every table poll.
- [x] Kept the column compact by only showing assigned users over the inactive threshold and rendering the full `username: N ngày` entry in red.
- [x] Verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_inactive_bot_alerts.py tests\test_bot_local.py`, `python -m compileall backend\app`, and `git diff --check`.

### 2026-05-12
- [x] Tạm tắt live normalize theo yêu cầu vận hành: default/source env chuyển sang `WORKER_LIVE_NORMALIZE_ENABLED=false`, còn toàn bộ profile `1080p/1440p/2160p` vẫn giữ nguyên để bật lại bằng env khi cần.
- [x] Thêm regression test xác nhận worker live mới không tự bật normalize nếu không set env.
- [x] Updated live normalize policy so `2160p/4K` sources keep original resolution instead of downscaling to `1440p`.
- [x] Added a `2160p` bitrate/CRF profile: `maxrate=20000 kbps`, `bufsize=40000 kbps` through the existing `maxrate * 2` rule, `CRF=21`, `preset=veryfast`, audio AAC `128 kbps`.
- [x] Kept GOP/keyframe cadence at 2 seconds (`-g` and `-keyint_min` computed from source FPS, `-sc_threshold 0`) to match YouTube ingest expectations.
- [x] Updated worker config, bootstrap env generation, deploy env examples, and regression tests for `4K no downscale`, `>4K cap to 2160p`, and `4K fast-path within 20 Mbps`.

### 2026-05-11
- [x] Investigated live streams that failed after entering `streaming`; all failures were FFmpeg RTMP output disconnects (`Broken pipe` / `End of file`) after YouTube ingest accepted data briefly.
- [x] Added live worker retry handling so transient RTMP output disconnects move to `disconnected` and reconnect instead of becoming terminal `error` immediately for streams without failover backup.
- [x] Preserved primary-to-backup failover: primary streams with `backup_worker_id` still stop and let backup take over instead of retrying locally.
- [x] Fixed live runtime lease refresh during guard checks so long prestream phases do not get reclaimed while the worker is still active.
- [x] Fixed reclaim logic so a started stream without backup can be claimed again from `disconnected` after worker restart.
- [x] Verified with `python -m compileall backend/app workers/agent`, `python -m unittest discover -s tests -p test_live_rtmp_retry.py`, and `python -m unittest discover -s tests -p test_live_runtime_lease.py`.

- [x] Added `docs/VPS_MIGRATION.md` and linked it from `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md`.
- [x] Recorded `DEC-043`: migrate the control-plane separately from BOT workers, then bootstrap/add workers after the new control-plane is verified.

Da sua `backend/app/worker_bootstrap.py` de coi case task da bi clear sau register la thanh cong hop le, khong crash nua, va them timing log `worker_bootstrap_step_completed` cho tung step `chmod / install-env / bootstrap` de lan sau co the xac dinh ngay bottleneck that tu journal ma khong phai suy doan.

- [2026-05-13] Polished the user dashboard Studio access action block so it uses the same bottom action-bar alignment as Render Job, keeps the button right-aligned with matching control sizing, and changes the helper copy to mention the saved channel Chrome profile. Verified with node --check backend/app/static/js/user_dashboard.js and python -m compileall backend/app.
- [2026-05-13] Adjusted the Studio access action block again per UI review: kept the divider and helper copy anchored at the bottom to match the render form action bar, but restored `Truy cập studio` to the original full-width button style instead of the compact right-aligned button.
- [2026-05-13] Fixed a Studio access modal race where `Đóng phiên` could render the channel picker and then a stale polling/create response immediately pushed the modal back to `Đang khởi tạo phiên Studio`. Added a state-version guard around Studio polling/create/delete, bumped the user dashboard JS cache key, and updated the add-channel red warning copy to point users to `Truy cập Studio` when Google shows `Verify it's you`.
- [2026-05-13] Investigated upload job `dreams67` / `job-333e32df` on `worker-08 / 94.72.127.28`: download/render reached 100% and upload reached ~50%, then Ubuntu `apt-daily-upgrade.service` upgraded `libavahi-*` and systemd stop/started `youtube-upload-worker.service`, causing control-plane to mark the progressed upload as error to avoid duplicate upload. Disabled unattended apt upgrades across 34 reachable worker profiles via stored SSH credentials (`apt-daily*` masked, `unattended-upgrades` disabled, `APT::Periodic` set to 0) without restarting worker services, and updated `scripts/bootstrap_worker.sh` so newly installed upload/live/backup BOTs apply the same guard during bootstrap.
- [2026-05-13] Added BOT list Excel export from `/admin/ManagerBOT/index`: toolbar action `Xuất DS BOT`, route `/admin/ManagerBOT/export`, stdlib `.xlsx` writer, manager-scoped export rows, and regression coverage for SSH user/password/local/status mapping.
- [2026-05-13] Removed the hard 5-thread ceiling for live/backup VPS quota allocation. `backend/app/store.py` no longer clamps live thread counts to 5, create/update/conversion routes in `backend/app/routers/web.py` and `backend/app/routers/api_admin.py` now bootstrap live worker `capacity` from the granted quota instead of a fixed cap, and the admin BOT modal in `backend/app/templates/admin/worker_index.html` drops the `max=5` input clamp plus the old “tối đa 5 luồng/VPS” copy. Added a regression test in `tests/test_bot_local.py` covering `6 x 2 user = 12` granted live quota.
- [2026-05-13] Hotfixed the live quota rollout after production logs showed `AttributeError: 'LiveWorkerHeartbeatPayload' object has no attribute 'capacity'`. `backend/app/store.py::heartbeat_live_worker()` now uses the heartbeat `threads` field (and optional `capacity` only if present) when reconciling live worker capacity, and `tests/test_bot_local.py` adds a regression test for heartbeat updates without a `capacity` field.
- [2026-05-13] Fixed BOT deletion for VPS records whose server can no longer be reached, including the legacy `82.197.71.6` app case. `worker_bootstrap._run_worker_decommission_operation()` now treats only SSH connection failures (`Không kết nối được VPS ...`) as safe local-record cleanup, calls `finalize_decommissioned_bot(..., reason_override="ssh_unreachable")`, and still fails normally for reachable-host cleanup/auth errors so real VPS services are not silently orphaned.
- [2026-05-13] Started the live local-supervisor hardening after production live disconnect analysis. Added `workers/agent/live_supervisor.py` for durable per-stream local state/events/ffmpeg logs under `worker-data/live-state/<stream_id>/`, made live progress reporting tolerate temporary control-plane POST failures, and changed control-plane heartbeat/lease expiry for live streams to `Mất telemetry` instead of self-triggered `disconnected/error` failover. Added regression coverage in `tests/test_live_runtime_lease.py` and `tests/test_live_supervisor.py`.
- [2026-05-13] Followed up after production rollout showed two streams stuck as `Mất telemetry` with claims still held by their original workers after service restart. Added a same-worker self-reclaim path that bypasses the normal capacity gate only for telemetry-stale streams already claimed by that worker, then covered it with `test_same_worker_can_reclaim_telemetry_stale_stream_after_restart`.
- [2026-05-13] Closed the main remaining risk in the live local-supervisor model: primary streams with configured backup now keep the conservative `Mất telemetry` state briefly, but after `LIVE_TELEMETRY_FAILOVER_SECONDS=180` they are escalated to `disconnected`, their claim is released for backup takeover, and ops Telegram is sent to admin/manager. Added `telemetry_stale_at` to `LiveStreamRecord`, regression tests for backup failover vs primary-only self-recovery, and `WORKER_LIVE_STATE_RETENTION_HOURS=168` cleanup for `worker-data/live-state/*`.
- [2026-05-13] Production deploy of the telemetry failover patch exposed a control-plane throughput bottleneck: Uvicorn access logs, repeated live `streaming` progress snapshots, live/upload heartbeat snapshots, and no-op claim polls were writing heavily while many workers reconnected, causing `/api/health` to timeout. Patched the systemd unit/bootstrap host script to start Uvicorn with `--no-access-log`, throttled persisted `streaming` progress snapshots with `LIVE_PROGRESS_SAVE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=120`, throttled live/upload heartbeat snapshots with `LIVE_WORKER_HEARTBEAT_SAVE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=300` and `WORKER_HEARTBEAT_SAVE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=300`, and removed writes from no-op live/upload claim polls while keeping in-memory state and leases fresh on every request.
- [2026-05-13] Followed up after live deploy measurements showed a post-restart write burst from first unchanged heartbeat/progress samples across many workers. Changed the throttle path so the first unchanged sample seeds the in-memory throttle timestamp without writing `app_state.db`; state transitions, job/stream claims, failover, stop/complete/error, and later checkpoint intervals still persist immediately or on schedule.
- [2026-05-13] Continued tracing the remaining write load and found upload job progress still persisted every download/render/upload tick. Added `WORKER_JOB_PROGRESS_SAVE_INTERVAL_SECONDS=120` so repeated same-status upload progress updates only checkpoint periodically, with tests covering same-status progress still updating in memory without a DB write.
- [2026-05-13] Ran gated `_save_state()` tracing in production and found the remaining write pressure still came from `update_live_stream_progress`: per-stream throttling was ineffective with many concurrent streams because each stream had its own checkpoint clock. Switched repeated `streaming` live progress to a single global checkpoint clock while keeping status-change persistence immediate.
- [2026-05-14] Reworked live telemetry/log volume for a larger fleet target. Repeated active live statuses, including `disconnected`, now share the global control-plane checkpoint path; worker-side live progress reporting is coarser (status/force still immediate, routine progress at up to 60s cadence), and local supervisor log caps were reduced from 5MB/10MB to 1MB/2MB per current+rotated event/ffmpeg file.
- [2026-05-14] Added worker API jitter/backoff tuning for scale-up. `workers/agent/main.py` now jitters heartbeat/poll sleeps and throttles no-op live claim probes while busy; `workers/agent/control_plane.py` adds retry jitter plus retry-log throttling; bootstrap env files include the new knobs so newly installed BOTs inherit the same behavior.
- [2026-05-14] Followed up the recent audit by widening live RTMP retry tokens without changing upload workers. `workers/agent/live_runner.py` now retries FFmpeg failures that expose `RTMP output disconnected`, `Connection reset by peer`, or muxer submit errors even when `Error writing trailer` is absent, and the live busy no-op claim interval default was lowered from 30s to 10s for faster multi-stream pickup.
- [2026-05-14] Diagnosed admin tab navigation jank as frontend style delivery rather than backend load: `_layout.html` used `cdn.tailwindcss.com` runtime plus blocking CDN Lucide in the head, so large table pages could render unstyled/oversized before CSS/table enhancement completed. Added `tailwind.admin.config.js`, built `backend/app/static/css/admin-tailwind.css`, and replaced the full Lucide bundle with `backend/app/static/vendor/lucide-admin.min.js` containing only admin-used icons. Follow-up hotfix removed the temporary table-hiding boot state because it caused blank white table panels while JS loaded, switched the icon bundle to deterministic `defer` loading, and replaced invalid `h-4.5/w-4.5` admin icon classes with generated Tailwind sizes. Verified `node --check backend/app/static/js/admin_tables.js`, `python -m compileall backend/app`, `git diff --check`, and local static smoke checks.
- [2026-05-14] Further reduced the remaining admin first-paint font/layout snap by self-hosting the admin font stack. Downloaded local WOFF2 assets for `Be Vietnam Pro`, `Inter`, and `IBM Plex Mono`, added `backend/app/static/css/admin-fonts.css` with `font-display: block`, removed the Google Fonts stylesheet/preconnect from the admin layout, and kept the local font CSS ahead of the Tailwind bundle so browser navigation no longer paints a large fallback font before switching to the final admin typography.
- [2026-05-14] Reduced client polling pressure from open dashboard tabs. `worker_index.html` switched the BOT list refresher from fixed `setInterval(3000)` to adaptive `setTimeout` with jitter, `user_dashboard.js` now polls active upload/browser work every ~2s but backs off to ~10s idle and ~30s hidden, and `user_live_dashboard.html` backs off live list refreshes to ~15s when no active rows need close tracking. Verified with `node --check backend/app/static/js/user_dashboard.js` and `python -m compileall backend/app`.
- [2026-05-14] Fixed the user-management avatar regression introduced by the static Tailwind bundle. `_avatar_palette()` returns dynamic classes such as `bg-violet-500`, `bg-fuchsia-500`, `bg-teal-500`, `bg-emerald-500`, and `bg-amber-500`; added those classes to `tailwind.admin.config.js`, rebuilt `backend/app/static/css/admin-tailwind.css`, and bumped the admin CSS cache key so user rows and manager picker options render colored initials again.
- [2026-05-14] Aligned upload `Verify it's you` Telegram copy with the Studio access feature. `_summarize_job_upload_error_message()` now tells the user to open `Truy cập Studio`, select the channel, verify the saved Chrome profile on the VPS, and rerun the job instead of deleting and re-adding the channel. Updated the upload interruption regression test accordingly.
- [2026-05-18] Tuned upload-worker auxiliary polling after checking production had 42 upload workers and only one active upload job. Added dedicated intervals for browser-session and decommission polling while leaving `claim_job` and heartbeat responsiveness unchanged; verified with focused pytest and compileall.
- [2026-05-29] Added per-user idle visibility to the admin BOT table. `backend/app/store.py` now builds a cached last-activity map for upload/live allocations and emits `inactive_users` only for users over the inactive threshold; `worker_index.html` renders the new `Không hoạt động` column after `Ngày tạo` as red `username: N ngày` lines. Verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_inactive_bot_alerts.py tests\test_bot_local.py`, `python -m compileall backend/app`, and `git diff --check`.
- [2026-05-30] Added a red bitrate guidance note to the user live form next to `Link video nguồn`, matching the existing small inline warning style used by the scheduling fields.
- [2026-05-30] Added `Local` into BOT offline/reconnected Telegram message builders for both upload and live workers, with `-` fallback when no region is configured. Covered the upload offline formatter with focused unit tests.
- [2026-05-30] Reworked live RTMP continuity after comparing the legacy `LiveStreamBOT-master` behavior and manual FFmpeg tests. `workers/agent/live_runner.py` now streams `rendered.flv` through one FFmpeg process with `-stream_loop -1 -c copy -f flv -flvflags no_duration_filesize`, so normal media looping does not close/reopen the RTMP session. Timed live streams with backup now keep primary and backup as independent parallel streams that may both retry RTMP until `end_time_live`, while `24/7` backup remains hot-standby/failover. Removed the temporary red bitrate warning from the user live form because the runtime command now avoids the previous per-loop RTMP restart pattern.
- [2026-05-30] Restored the red bitrate warning beside `Link video nguồn` in the user live form after production/manual checks still showed YouTube bitrate warnings for high-bitrate sources. This is a UI copy-only change; runtime live workers are unchanged.
- [2026-05-30] Reworked live list job information to match the upload job table without loading source videos in the browser. Live workers now capture a small JPEG preview from the downloaded video source and upload it to control-plane; user/admin live tables show that cached thumbnail plus stream/user info, merge live/backup BOT values into one `Bot` column, and avoid direct 4K video preview URLs.
- [2026-05-31] Tightened live RTMP retry to match the legacy app's near-immediate reconnect behavior. `workers/agent/live_runner.py` now defaults `WORKER_LIVE_RTMP_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS=0`, so transient YouTube/RTMPS resets reconnect after FFmpeg exits and respawns instead of waiting a fixed cooldown. Timed live streams with backup still keep primary and backup parallel, but control-plane suppresses disconnect/backup-activated Telegram noise for that timed-parallel branch. Verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_live_rtmp_retry.py tests\test_live_runtime_lease.py`, `python -m compileall backend\app workers\agent`, and `git diff --check`.
- [2026-05-31] Followed up on live notification noise: `backend/app/store.py` no longer sends Telegram live messages for runtime disconnect/retry or backup-activated transitions across `24/7` and timed streams. Start/end lifecycle messages remain; BOT/control-plane telemetry alerts stay on the ops Telegram path. Added regression coverage for `24/7` primary disconnect and backup activation staying silent.
- [2026-05-31] Tuned `24/7` backup failover latency after comparing it with the old app's backup check window and YouTube auto-stop risk. `LIVE_TELEMETRY_FAILOVER_SECONDS` now defaults to `120s` instead of `180s`; with the existing fast live lease this keeps transient telemetry jitter conservative but lets hot-standby backup take over sooner if the primary worker/VPS is truly unreachable. This is a control-plane-only change, so live/upload workers do not need rollout.
- [2026-05-31] Prepared a scoped FIFO recovery live test for stream key `bm1e-pjq1-272q-5ts3-1g9y`. Added `WORKER_LIVE_RTMP_FIFO_ENABLED=true` as an opt-in worker flag, leaving the normal RTMP command as default. The FIFO command uses explicit `-map 0:v:0 -map 0:a:0`, `-fifo_format flv`, recovery options, and `-format_opts flvflags=no_duration_filesize`; syntax was validated on `62.146.168.126` against the existing `rendered.flv`.
- [2026-05-31] Followed up the FIFO test after restarting the two test live workers to apply `WORKER_LIVE_RTMP_FIFO_QUEUE_SIZE=600` exposed a recovery gap: the worker process came back, but control-plane still held an active `streaming` lease and the new process did not immediately reclaim/start FFmpeg. Patched live heartbeat reconcile so an active live worker reporting no `active_stream_ids` marks its own active streams `Mất telemetry` immediately, allowing same-worker claim/restart without waiting for lease expiry. Verified with live lease regression tests.
- [2026-06-01] Added the alternate SSH-key path for admin BOT install. The create-BOT modal now has a `+ Nhập SSH key` switch that replaces password input with a private-key textarea while keeping Local/group/manager/user settings unchanged. API/web create handlers normalize `auth_mode`, existing saved connection profiles support decommission with `ssh_private_key`, and `tests/test_bot_local.py` now verifies decommission requests use the saved private key instead of password.
- [2026-06-01] Polished the create-BOT SSH-key modal after UI review. The modal now keeps its scrollbar inside the rounded panel, the auth switch no longer shows a leading plus, and SSH-key mode clears the default `root` value so the `SSH user` field can show a username-only placeholder such as `ubuntu`.
- [2026-06-01] Fixed the SSH-key BOT install failure UX. `fail_worker_operation()` now marks failed install/decommission tasks as `failed` instead of removing them, so failed install rows remain visible with the `Cài đặt lỗi` badge and exact SSH/backend message until the user retries the same VPS/worker. The create/install path also normalizes `ssh ubuntu@host` or `ubuntu@host` into `ubuntu` before building/storing the SSH profile.
- [2026-06-02] Ran production live retry verification without restarting workers. Tested timed stream `live-a17c5ac3b6` on primary `live-worker-54` and backup clone `live-b5b1f2866e` on `live-worker-59`, plus `24/7` primary `live-24bb0ee799` on `live-worker-13`. Each FFmpeg process was killed with `SIGTERM`, and each local supervisor respawned a new one-pass FFmpeg command within roughly `0.6-1.0s`; control-plane state returned to `streaming` with no stale telemetry.
- [2026-06-02] Started a local-only admin UI smoothness pass. Added `backend/app/static/js/admin_navigation.js` to prefetch admin sidebar/module pages and use warmed HTML for fast document replacement on click, then wired it into `admin/_layout.html` with a subtle content opacity transition. Verified `node --check` for the new script and `admin_tables.js`, plus `python -m compileall backend/app`; started local Uvicorn on `127.0.0.1:8000` for browser testing.
- [2026-06-02] Followed up local browser testing by including fast navigation on the two workspace pages themselves. `admin_navigation.js` now whitelists `/app` and `/app/live`, `user_dashboard.html` and `user_live_dashboard.html` load it directly, and the live workspace form/search controls have scoped focus styling to avoid default black browser borders after prefetched document replacement.
- [2026-06-02] Fixed the fast navigation hydration regression reported after production rollout. The prefetcher no longer prevents default navigation or writes prefetched HTML into the current document; it only warms eligible admin/workspace links and lets the browser perform the route change, preserving Lucide initialization, table action handlers, and page modal scripts.
- [2026-06-02] Bumped all `admin_navigation.js` template references to `?v=20260602-safe-real-nav` after confirming the production domain could still serve the older cached script with the previous query string.
- [2026-06-02] Added a `24/7` live hot-standby failover path for primary FFmpeg health, separate from worker telemetry. `workers/agent/live_runner.py` now reports `runtime_health=rtmp_unhealthy` when a primary with backup has no FFmpeg progress or repeated RTMP retry failures beyond `WORKER_LIVE_PRIMARY_UNHEALTHY_SECONDS`, and reports `healthy` when progress resumes. `backend/app/store.py` records `rtmp_unhealthy_at` and lets backup stream after `LIVE_PRIMARY_HEALTH_FAILOVER_SECONDS`, then returns it to standby when the primary recovers.
- [2026-06-15] Investigated stuck production BOT decommission for upload `worker-40` (`158.220.94.36`): the saved task failed with `[Errno 104] Connection reset by peer` and the host was no longer reachable on SSH, so the control-plane record was removed with `delete_bot_without_ssh()` while `youtube-upload-web.service` was stopped to avoid state overwrite. Patched decommission handling so transport resets/no-route errors tombstone local records and failed decommission tasks can be retried instead of blocking deletion. Verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_bot_local.py`, `python -m compileall backend\app`, and `/api/health` on production.
- [2026-06-15] Fixed inactive-BOT Telegram false positives. `backend/app/store.py` now excludes tombstoned/deleted workers by worker id/name/VPS IP before building inactive snapshots or daily alert records, and skips live primary/backup allocations that currently have a non-terminal live stream (`scheduled`, `downloading`, `preparing`, `waiting`, `streaming`, or `disconnected`). Added regression coverage for deleted BOT aliases and active live/backup streams; verified with `python -m pytest tests\test_inactive_bot_alerts.py` and `python -m compileall backend\app`.
- [2026-06-22] Investigated production upload BOT `worker-03` (`62.146.168.88`) after a 23h30 render failed with `FFmpeg failed (228)`: control-plane DB showed FFmpeg reached `out_time=23:09:08.023289` and `total_size=148705902640` before `No space left on device`; the BOT disk was back to `139G` free after worker cleanup, confirming the render output size exceeded the 145G VPS disk rather than a broken source or dead worker.
- [2026-06-22] Resized production BOT `worker-03` (`62.146.168.88`) after Contabo SSD expansion: fixed the GPT backup table with `sgdisk -e /dev/sda`, expanded `/dev/sda1` with `growpart /dev/sda 1`, and online-resized ext4 with `resize2fs /dev/sda1`, increasing `/` from `145G` to `290G` without restarting the worker. Confirmed `job-de7116c7` was still active (`uploading`); a temporary post-job restart watcher was started and then canceled after confirming restart was unnecessary for the resized filesystem.
- [2026-06-24] Investigated pending production job `job-c428093d` on upload BOT `worker-10` (`62.169.23.112`). The BOT heartbeat remained online, but `py-spy` showed its main thread still blocked inside `gdown.download()` with `timeout=None` for orphaned job `job-76b493f8`, while the heartbeat thread continued independently. Confirmed the stale job no longer existed in control-plane and the new job had not been claimed, then safely restarted only `youtube-upload-worker.service`; startup janitor removed the stale job directory and the new job moved from `pending` to `downloading` under `worker-10`.
- [2026-06-24] Hardened remote downloads after the `worker-10` incident. `workers/agent/downloader.py` now runs `gdown` as a cancellable subprocess, monitors both final target and gdown `*.part` growth, terminates stalled/cancelled downloads, removes partial files, retries 3 times by default, and applies finite HTTP read timeouts to direct fallback downloads. Verified with 5 focused downloader tests, full `123 passed`, and worker compileall. Pushed commits `5c6d9e2` + `dab6103`; fast-forwarded production control-plane source without restarting web, deployed canonical blob hash `2719d37d15d1b50645340414fcd421346f208523e4b3f49eb3c28842a6e5ed85` to all 32 upload BOTs, restarted 28 idle BOTs immediately, and installed a control-plane watcher for 4 busy BOTs. The watcher subsequently restarted `worker-21` after its upload completed while leaving `worker-10`, `worker-20`, and `worker-28` untouched during active jobs.
- [2026-06-29] Investigated a Telegram inactive-BOT alert that still listed deleted/stale BOT IPs (`158.220.91.85`, `46.250.243.199`, `46.250.246.31`, `154.26.159.8`) using the old per-user message format. Current production on `109.123.227.253` is running commit `50a1080`, `store.py` only contains the BOT-level `User được cấp` formatter, and today's DB snapshot marks `worker-01`, `worker-04`, and `live-worker-04` as `inactive_days_alert=false` while `worker-05` is tombstoned. The actual stale sender was historical host `82.197.71.6`: `youtube-upload-web.service` was still active on commit `914a9e8`, with runtime DB updated at the 08:00 VN alert window and the old `User: ... | Manager: ...` formatter. Stopped and disabled that service; current production remained active and healthy on `109.123.227.253`.
- [2026-07-16] Fixed the inactive-BOT Telegram false positive where a long-running 24/7 live pair appeared immediately as 47 days idle after being manually stopped. Telegram selection now counts terminal live inactivity from `ended_at` / `stop_requested_at` / fallback `updated_at`, while non-terminal streams remain exempt and the admin BOT snapshot/upload behavior stay unchanged. `stop_live_stream()` now logs the actor to the control-plane journal without adding actor details to Telegram. Verified focused `14 passed`, full `137 passed`, and backend compileall.
- [2026-07-17] Diagnosed two apparent inactive-BOT conflicts from production state. `live-worker-28` was correctly inactive at the 08:00 Telegram check and only became busy when a new stream was created at 09:53; `worker-01` had already moved to manager `trieudo` with zero assignments, but the admin table still overlaid the 08:00 `huong/hung` snapshot. Updated table composition to retain stable daily values only while current assignment/activity remains compatible, clearing stale positives after a new job/stream or assignment change. Added focused regressions for both cases.
- [2026-08-03] Diagnosed production control-plane saturation on `109.123.227.253`: Uvicorn held one CPU core near 100%, `/api/health` timed out, and `py-spy` traced the hot path through live progress policy/lease work. With 939 retained live records, backup lookup rescanned the list, 426 settled timed streams were re-finalized with rewritten `ended_at`, existing backup clones were re-upserted on routine telemetry, and runtime-state lease refresh rescanned all streams. Added one-pass indexes, stable-terminal short-circuiting, no-op clone refresh mode for runtime traffic, and direct lease refresh on the authenticated stream, with regressions for each behavior and no worker/API contract change.
- [2026-08-03] Added control-plane history bounds after the live-history saturation audit. Admin upload/live lists now use server-side search and 20-row pagination. A daily retention pass removes only upload `completed` records older than 30 days and genuinely terminal live `ended` primary/backup pairs with no live flag, claim, or lease; active/waiting/retrying/error records remain. This change does not alter worker runtime or FFmpeg.
- [2026-08-03] Fixed the server-paginated admin table header regression found during production browser verification. Upload/live headers now keep the standard padded header-label markup without exposing client-side sort controls that would only reorder the current 20-row page.
- [2026-08-19] Fixed inactive-BOT age drift after history retention. Control-plane now persists activity per `user + BOT + workspace/role`, preserving the last upload/live terminal time before completed-history cleanup and separating prior-user activity from new assignments. Added a one-off SQLite snapshot backfill tool for activity already purged before the watermark existed; worker runtime remains unchanged.
- [2026-08-19] Made the BOT table refresh its daily inactive snapshot when a corrected activity watermark differs from the stored snapshot. This updates the admin table after a backfill without re-sending the already-delivered Telegram alert.
